Result: 实验揭示了即使是最好的VLM与人类之间存在显著性能差距。大多数模型难以准确感知视觉文本，导致频繁的纠正错误。

Conclusion: ReViCo为开发更强大、更具文本感知能力的VLM提供了新的基准基础，有助于推动该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown great success in general visual tasks, yet they still struggle to deeply understand text within images. In this paper, we introduce ReViCo (Real Visual Correction), a benchmark designed to evaluate VLM text understanding through a novel task of visual text error correction. ReViCo challenges models to identify and fix text errors in real-world images, which requires a profound understanding of the interplay between visual text and its surrounding visual context. We benchmark various VLMs using two distinct paradigms: prompt-based strategy and targeted model training, both aimed at pushing the limits of current models. Our experiments reveal a striking performance gap between even the best VLMs and human, and further analysis also shows that most models struggle to accurately perceive the visual text, resulting in frequent correction errors. By highlighting these gaps, ReViCo provides a new benchmark foundation for developing more robust and text-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Magpie: Real-Time World Renderer for Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27168)
*Xiaoyu Zhan,Xinyu Wang,Xiaohong Zhang,Huanjie Zhu,Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Jiaxing Yu,Yanwen Guo,Dongjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Magpie是一个实时生成式游戏世界渲染系统，将游戏逻辑执行与视觉生成分离。设计师在游戏引擎中定义场景和规则，游戏引擎在运行时解析玩家动作并维护世界状态，独立的渲染服务器从引擎生成的白框帧生成视觉输出。该系统为生成模型在实时游戏渲染中的应用提供了系统级实现路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏开发依赖传统图形管线，高质量视觉内容需要建模、材质创作、动画、灯光、特效和运行时优化，使得资产制作昂贵并延长游戏原型的开发周期。视频基础模型开始改变影视制作，但游戏与线性媒体不同，不仅需要连续逼真的图像，还需要稳定可复现的游戏规则、物体状态和交互结果。早期游戏原型高度依赖完整的视觉资产。

Method: 提出Magpie实时生成式世界渲染系统，将游戏逻辑执行与视觉生成分离。设计师在游戏引擎中定义场景和规则，运行时由游戏引擎解析玩家动作并维护世界状态，独立的渲染服务器从引擎产生的白框帧生成视觉输出。

Result: 提供了将生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染的系统级实现路径；保留了游戏可设计性和可复现性；降低了早期游戏原型对完整视觉资产的依赖。

Conclusion: Magpie成功实现了交互式游戏的实时生成式渲染，通过分离视觉生成与游戏逻辑，为游戏开发提供了新的技术路径，有望显著降低游戏开发成本和周期，使原型开发不再高度依赖完整的视觉资产。

Abstract: Modern game development relies heavily on conventional graphics pipelines. High-quality visual content requires modeling, material authoring, animation, lighting, effects, and runtime optimization, making asset production expensive and extending the development cycle of game prototypes. Recently, video foundation models are beginning to change film and video production, but games differ from linear media, they require not only continuous and realistic imagery, but also stable and reproducible gameplay rules, object states, and interaction outcomes. We present Magpie, a real-time generative world-rendering system for interactive games. Magpie separates gameplay execution from visual generation. Designers define scenes and rules in a game engine. At runtime, the Game Engine resolves player actions and maintains world state, while an independent Render Server generates visual output from white-box frames produced by the engine. Magpie provides a system-level implementation path for applying generative models to real-time game rendering. It preserves gameplay designability and reproducibility, and reduces the dependence of early game prototypes on complete visual assets.

</details>


### [73] [Ancient-Bench: A Comprehensive Multi-millennial, Multi-medium, and Multi-script Benchmark for Ancient Chinese Artifact Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27169)
*Hiuyi Cheng,Nuo Xu,Yuyi Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Wei Pan,Jing Zhang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Yihua Teng,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ancient-Bench，一个用于古汉字文物文本识别的大规模基准数据集，包含2700张图像，涵盖3000年时间跨度、9种文物类别和7种历史书体形式，并设计了三种针对不同介质的标注标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古文字识别基准存在"碎片化"问题，具体表现为时间覆盖有限、介质多样性不足、书体类型不完整，导致难以全面评估模型在古汉字文物识别任务上的能力。

Method: 构建了多时间跨度（3000年）、多介质（9类文物）、多书体（7种古文字）的综合基准数据集；设计了符号标准化、字符标准化和解析标准化三种标注标准以适应不同介质特性；对通用视觉语言模型(VLM)和专业OCR模型进行了广泛实验评估。

Result: 实验表明古汉字文物文本识别仍是一个未解决的问题，模型在异体字、专业符号和幻觉问题上面临持续挑战。

Conclusion: Ancient-Bench基准数据集的提出为古汉字文物识别研究提供了全面评估工具，揭示了当前模型在该领域的局限性，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Ancient Chinese artifact text recognition is fundamental to heritage digitization, and benchmarks for ancient texts are essential for evaluating current model capabilities. However, existing benchmarks suffer from ''fragmentation'', manifested in limited temporal coverage, limited medium diversity, and incomplete script types. Therefore, we present Ancient-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 2,700 images for ancient Chinese artifact text recognition, featuring three dimensions: Multi-millennial (spanning 3,000 years of character evolution), Multi-medium (covering nine artifact categories), and Multi-script (encompassing seven historical script forms). To enable consistent and fair evaluation across heterogeneous media, we further define three annotation standards tailored to the medium-specific characteristics of ancient texts: symbol standardization, character standardization, and parsing standardization. Extensive experiments on Ancient-Bench covering general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and OCR-specialist models reveal that ancient Chinese artifact text recognition remains fundamentally unsolved, with persistent challenges in variant characters, specialized symbols, and hallucination. The dataset is available at https://github.com/SCUT-DLVCLab/Ancient_Bench.

</details>


### [74] [Temporal Sensitivity Analysis of Tessera Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27175)
*Julia Guerrero-Viu,Alex López-Cifuentes,Ignacio Pérez-Villar,Fabio Pacifici*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究 Earth Observation 基础模型 Tessera 在不同时间观测窗口（从一年到一天）下的土地覆盖/土地使用制图性能，发现模型价值因任务而异， temporal coverage 是可调成本而非固定前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最强的 Earth Observation 基础模型使用整年观测构建 embedding，但许多应用需要精确且频繁更新的土地覆盖图。需要研究时间敏感性以实现近实时制图和更快更新周期。

Method: 保持 encoder 冻结，在不同观测窗口（从一年到单天）重新计算 Tessera 的 embeddings，使用线性探测器和 UNet 分割头作为输入，在 LUCAS、DynamicEarthNet 和 PASTIS-R 数据集上与从头训练网络对比。

Result: PASTIS-R 作物类型分类中，基于 embedding 的模型达到 58.3 mIoU，比最好的从头模型高 46%；DynamicEarthNet 和 LUCAS 中森林等时间稳定类别，两种模型仅在全监督下相当。收缩窗口从一年到一个月，PASTIS-R 损失 39% 准确率，DynamicEarthNet 仅损失 5%。单天 embedding 仍可在 LUCAS 以 3.4 倍随机概率准确分类土地覆盖。

Conclusion: 时间覆盖是可调成本而非固定前提条件，为近实时制图和更快土地覆盖更新周期开辟了新路径。Embedding 模型在标签效率上始终更优。

Abstract: Many Earth Observation applications need land-use/land-cover maps that are both precise and frequently updated, yet the strongest Earth Observation foundation models build their embeddings from a full year of observations. We present a controlled study of the temporal sensitivity of Tessera, one of these leading foundation models, for land-use/land-cover mapping. Keeping the encoder frozen, we recompute its embeddings over varying observation windows, from a full year down to a single day. We use them as inputs to a linear probe and a UNet segmentation head, benchmarking both of them against from-scratch networks on LUCAS, DynamicEarthNet, and PASTIS-R datasets. We show that the value of the embeddings is task-dependent. Where classes are separated by phenology, as for the crop types of PASTIS-R, they reach a mean Intersection-over-Union of $58.3$, about $46\%$ above the best from-scratch model. Where classes are temporally stable (e.g., forests in DynamicEarthNet and LUCAS), embedding-based and from-scratch models match only under full supervision. On both datasets, Tessera embeddings remain markedly more label-efficient. Degradation under shorter temporal windows is gradual and class-dependent. Contracting the window from one year to one month costs $39\%$ of the segmentation accuracy on PASTIS-R but only $5\%$ on DynamicEarthNet. Single-day embeddings still classify land cover in LUCAS at $3.4$ times the chance level. Our study shows that temporal coverage is therefore a tunable cost rather than a fixed prerequisite, opening regimes such as near-real-time mapping and faster land-use/land-cover refresh cycles.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient pretrained-CT-to-MRI Transfer for Rectal Cancer Segmentation: Performance-Calibration Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27178)
*Aneesh Rangnekar,Jorge Tapias Gomez,Joseph O Deasy,Harini Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIFT是一种基于Swin Transformer的直肠癌MRI分割模型，采用参数高效微调和肿瘤感知微调方法。SWIFTe将参数减少70.1%（从7280万降至2180万），肿瘤检出率从89.9%提升至93.9%，中位表面DSC为0.61。SWIFTe-LDE4获得最低校准误差（ECE=0.217）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI直肠癌精准分割对自适应放疗和肿瘤反应评估至关重要，但临床部署需要计算效率和可靠的校准不确定性估计。

Method: 使用DINOv2风格目标在10444个公共3D CT体积上预训练的Swin V2编码器，通过四种累积配置适应T2加权MRI：完全微调(SWIFT)、解码器压缩(SWIFTe)、低秩适应(SWIFTe-LoRA)和四成员LoRA解码器集成(SWIFTe-LDE4)。在247例测试集上评估几何精度、肿瘤检出、放射组学一致性和概率校准。

Result: SWIFTe参数减少70.1%，肿瘤检出率从89.9%提升至93.9%，表面DSC为0.61（vs SWIFT 0.62），放疗组学一致性改善。去除肿瘤感知增强后检出降至89.9%但DSC升至0.64，展示检出-边界-一致性权衡。SWIFTe-LDE4校准误差最低（ECE=0.217，Brier=0.222）。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调策略可在保持分割性能同时显著减少参数量和改善不确定性校准，但当前结果仅限单中心数据，外部临床泛化性需进一步验证。

Abstract: Accurate rectal cancer segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for adaptive radiotherapy and tumor response assessment, but deployment also requires computational efficiency and informative, calibrated uncertainty estimates. We therefore introduce SWIFT, a SWin pretrained model wIth parameter-eFficient and Tumor-aware fine-tuning for rectal cancer segmentation. A Swin V2 encoder pretrained on 10,444 public 3D CT volumes using a DINOv2-style objective was adapted to T2-weighted MRI through four cumulative configurations: full fine-tuning (SWIFT), decoder compression (SWIFTe), low-rank adaptation (SWIFTe-LoRA), and a four-member LoRA-decoder ensemble (SWIFTe-LDE4). Geometric accuracy, tumor detection, radiomic agreement, and probability calibration were evaluated on a held-out 247-case test set from a single-institution cohort acquired using 1.5 or 3 Tesla GE scanners. Compared with SWIFT, SWIFTe reduced total parameters by 70.1% (from 72.8M to 21.8M) and increased tumor detection rate from 89.9% to 93.9%, while achieving a slightly lower median surface DSC (0.61 versus 0.62) and improved radiomic agreement. In a separate SWIFTe ablation, removing tumor-aware augmentation reduced detection from 93.9% to 89.9% but increased surface DSC from 0.61 to 0.64, demonstrating a detection-boundary-agreement trade-off. SWIFTe-LoRA used 14.6% of SWIFTe's trainable parameters while retaining similar segmentation performance. SWIFTe-LDE4 achieved the lowest calibration errors among the four configurations after temperature scaling (expected calibration error, 0.217; Brier score, 0.222), although the absolute expected calibration error indicates residual miscalibration. Similar efficiency-calibration patterns were observed using the public VoCo checkpoint, supporting robustness across pretrained initializations rather than external clinical generalizability.

</details>


### [76] [SSMB: Self-Supervised Local Feature Detection under Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27181)
*Zhenjun Zhao,Fabio Bellavia,Wenting Wang,Fan Zhu,Jiajun Wu,Suryansh Kumar,Mingqiang Wei,Haoang Li,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSMB是一个用于运动模糊图像的无去模糊、自监督关键点检测器，通过局部判别性增强(LDE)模块和两阶段训练（几何预训练+模糊感知训练）实现模糊不变的关键点检测，在关键点检测、图像匹配、相对姿态估计和视觉定位等任务上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动模糊会扭曲局部图像结构并降低特征定位的可重复性，现有方法要么依赖计算昂贵的去模糊后检测流程，要么学习回归手工关键点在清晰图像上的位置，无法真正适应模糊场景下的关键点检测。

Method: 提出SSMB方法，包含：(1)局部判别性增强(LDE)模块，在全局特征混合后恢复细粒度局部判别性；(2)两阶段训练：第一阶段在合成形状上进行几何预训练，第二阶段在真实清晰-模糊图像对上进行模糊感知训练，采用多组件自监督目标强制跨域一致性、几何对齐和空间覆盖。

Result: 在关键点检测、图像匹配、相对姿态估计和运动模糊下的视觉定位等任务上，SSMB始终优于所有监督和自监督基线方法，建立了稀疏关键点检测器的新技术标准。

Conclusion: SSMB是一种无需手工检测器或外部伪标签的端到端模糊关键点检测方法，通过自监督学习实现了模糊不变的关键点检测，为运动模糊场景下的视觉任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Keypoint detection under motion blur remains a significant challenge, as blur distorts local image structure and degrades the repeatability of feature localization. Existing approaches either rely on computationally expensive deblur-then-detect pipelines that may introduce restoration artifacts, or learn to regress the image positions of handcrafted keypoints extracted on sharp images, which reflects the assumptions of the handcrafted detector rather than what is truly repeatable under blur. We present SSMB, a deblur-free, self-supervised keypoint detector for motion-blurred images that requires neither handcrafted detectors nor external pseudo-labels. SSMB introduces the Local Discriminability Enhancement (LDE) module, which restores fine-grained local discriminability after global feature mixing. Training is performed in two stages. First, geometric pretraining on synthetic shapes bootstraps spatially discriminative keypoint detection without any external detector, just from the rendered geometry. Second, blur-aware training on real sharp-blur image pairs learns blur-invariant detection through a multi-component self-supervised objective that enforces cross-domain consistency, geometric alignment, and spatial coverage. Extensive evaluations on keypoint detection, image matching, relative pose estimation, and visual localization under motion blur demonstrate that SSMB establishes a new state-of-the-art among sparse keypoint detectors, consistently outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines across all tasks. Code, models, and datasets will be publicly available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [77] [Unsupervised Adaptation of 3D CT Foundation Models for 3D CBCT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27190)
*Gauthier Miralles,Loic Le Folgoc,Vincent Jugnon,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于冗余减少特征对齐的无监督域适应框架，用于在没有目标域标注的情况下实现CBCT的3D分割。该方法架构无关，可同时适配CNN和ViT基础模型，在两个CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准上表现优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的CBCT 3D分割对介入和放疗应用至关重要，但面临两大挑战：标注CBCT数据稀缺以及与诊断CT之间的大域迁移。介入性CBCT与常规CT存在根本性的模态差异，限制了跨模态模型迁移的有效性。

Method: 提出一种基于冗余减少特征对齐的新型无监督域适配(UDA)框架，无需目标域标注或推理时适应即可实现3D CBCT分割。该框架架构无关，可无缝适配CNN和ViT基础模型。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准（介入血管手术和放疗）上进行评估，表明即使是大规模预训练的分割网络也需要显式的特征空间桥接才能跨采集模态泛化，且该方法始终优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练的分割网络需要显式的特征空间桥接才能跨采集模态泛化。所提方法在两个基准上始终优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略，并发布了公开CBCT数据集的肝脏分割、代码、训练模型和权重。

Abstract: Accurate 3D segmentation of cone-beam CT (CBCT) is critical for interventional and radiation therapy applications, yet it remains limited by two compounding challenges: the scarcity of annotated CBCT data and the large domain shift from diagnostic CT. Interventional CBCT exhibits fundamental modality differences from conventional CT, driven by acquisition and physics effects as well as contrast-specific vascular content, thereby limiting effective cross-modality model transfer. We propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework based on redundancy-reducing feature alignment, enabling 3D CBCT segmentation with no target-domain annotations or inference-time adaptation. Our framework is architecture-agnostic, seamlessly adapting both CNN-based and ViT-based foundation models. We evaluate our method on two challenging CT-CBCT liver segmentation benchmarks: one for interventional vascular procedures and one for radiation therapy, demonstrating that even large-scale pretrained segmentation networks require explicit feature-space bridging to generalize across acquisition modalities, and that our approach consistently outperforms existing pretrained foundation model and UDA strategies. To support reproducibility and benchmarking, we release the liver segmentations for a public CBCT dataset, along with the code, trained models, and weights.

</details>


### [78] [Vision-centric generative AI models: A software-hardware perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27199)
*Eleni Tselepi,Cristian Sestito,Shady Agwa,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于视觉生成式AI在边缘设备上部署的Perspective文章，探讨了当前生成式AI模型参数成本高、能耗大的问题，倡导软硬件协同设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成式AI虽发展迅速，但大模型主要在数据中心运行，无法满足自动驾驶、农业传感器、移动设备等边缘场景的硬件约束需求。

Method: 作者量化分析了多种生成式模型在不同加速器平台上的参数成本和能效，并将四类生成模型家族与七个实际应用领域进行映射对比。

Result: 当前视觉生成式AI的发展以输出质量为导向，硬件是被动适应模型需求；需要在设计初期就考虑部署约束，实现"合适的模型"运行在"合适的硬件"上服务于"合适的应用"。

Conclusion: 倡导软硬件协同设计方法，使生成式AI部署更加可持续，并在更广泛的平台上实现可访问性。

Abstract: Vision generative artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as one of the most rapidly advancing areas of deep learning. The explosion of multimodal models has made them widely associated with text-to-image applications running on large datacentres. However, vision generative models are equally needed in applications that operate under strict hardware constraints at the edge, including autonomous vehicles, agricultural sensors, and mobile devices. In this Perspective, we argue that progress in vision generative AI has been driven by output quality, with hardware evolving reactively to accommodate growing model demands. We quantify the parameter cost and energy efficiency of these models across a range of accelerator platforms, and map four generative model families against seven real-world application domains. Finally, we advocate a software-hardware co-design approach, where deployment constraints are considered from the start of the design process, ensuring that the "right model" runs on the "right hardware" to serve the "right application", making generative AI deployment sustainable and accessible across a much broader range of platforms.

</details>


### [79] [PACE: A Unified Condense-and-Extract Paradigm for Fast VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27206)
*Junjie Liu,Shengyuan Ye,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE是一个无需训练的推理加速框架，通过统一"压缩-提取"范式同时加速视觉编码器和LLM推理，解决VLM中视觉token过多导致的推理成本高和性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法存在两大根本局限：1）仅在视觉编码器后操作，未优化编码阶段的大量延迟；2）在严格token预算下难以同时保留整体视觉上下文和细粒度细节，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出PACE框架，包含两个阶段：1）Condense阶段：使用自适应像素压缩器(APC)在编码前评估视觉信息密度，自适应下采样冗余输入，在保留全局上下文和关键视觉线索的同时减少编码计算；2）Extract阶段：使用动态双注意力提取器(DDAE)结合编码器的内部视觉信号和LLM的语义信号，选择性保留视觉token，保护任务关键细节。

Result: 将PACE集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B后，模型仅使用10%的视觉token即可保留93.8%的原始性能，TTFT（首个token生成时间）提升3.1倍。

Conclusion: PACE通过训练-free的"压缩-提取"范式成功实现了VLM视觉编码和LLM推理的双重加速，为高效视觉语言推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate exceptional visual reasoning capabilities, yet their inference costs escalate rapidly with the proliferation of visual tokens. Existing visual token pruning methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. First, most approaches operate exclusively post-vision encoder, leaving the substantial latency of the visual encoding phase unoptimized. Second, under strict token budgets, these methods often fail to jointly preserve holistic visual contexts and fine-grained details, leading to performance degradation. To address these bottlenecks, we propose PACE (Pixel-Adaptive Condense and Extract), a training-free inference framework that accelerates both the vision encoder and the Large Language Model (LLM) via a unified Condense-and-Extract paradigm. During the Condense stage, an Adaptive Pixel Compressor (APC) evaluates visual information density prior to encoding, adaptively downsampling redundant inputs, curtailing encoder computation while preserving global context and essential visual cues. In the Extract stage, a Dynamic Dual-Attention Extractor (DDAE) selectively retains visual tokens via a fusion of internal visual signals from the encoder and semantic signals from the LLM, safeguarding task-critical details. By integrating PACE into Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the model retains 93.8% of its original performance while utilizing only 10% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.1x speedup in time to first token (TTFT). Our code is available at https://github.com/jjL357/PACE.

</details>


### [80] [CODE: Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression for Open World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27214)
*Hao Xu,Zhaoning Shi,Hehe Jin,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CODE框架通过跨模态置信度校准、不确定性引导的通用目标增强和动态异常抑制三项技术，解决开放世界目标检测中语义歧义和过度抑制未知类别的问题，在OWOD任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 多模态基础模型在开放世界目标检测中存在语义歧义问题，由单向文本-视觉匹配导致；2. 刚性异常惩罚会过度抑制决策边界附近的未知物体

Method: 1. Cross-Modal Joint Confidence Calibration：注入全局视觉原型校准文本驱动的已知类别预测；2. Uncertainty-Guided Universal Objectness Enhancement：测量局部视觉响应的分类犹豫度以增强潜在未知物体；3. Dynamic Outlier Suppression via Confidence Margin：采用边际感知调整替代刚性抑制，保留模糊的分布外实例

Result: 在Real-World Detection基准上使用OWL-ViT L/14骨干网，CODE在Task 1达到21.7 U-mAP和40.8 K-mAP

Conclusion: CODE统一推理框架在开放世界目标检测任务上超越先前最先进水平，分别提升2.6和2.3个百分点

Abstract: Open World Object Detection (OWOD) built on multimodal foundation models often suffers from semantic ambiguity caused by unidirectional text-to-vision matching, while rigid outlier penalties may over-suppress unknown objects near known-class decision boundaries. We propose CODE (Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression), a unified inference-time framework with three complementary components. Cross-Modal Joint Confidence Calibration injects global visual prototypes to calibrate text-driven known-class predictions. Uncertainty-Guided Universal Objectness Enhancement measures classification hesitation from local visual responses to strengthen potential unknown objects. Dynamic Outlier Suppression via Confidence Margin replaces rigid suppression with a margin-aware adjustment that preserves ambiguous out-of-distribution instances. Experiments on the Real-World Detection benchmark demonstrate that, with the OWL-ViT L/14 backbone, CODE achieves 21.7 U-mAP and 40.8 K-mAP in Task 1, surpassing the previous state of the art by 2.6 and 2.3 points, respectively.

</details>


### [81] [DINOcular: Self-Supervised Visuospatial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27226)
*Farkhat Almukhamedov,Sami Azirar,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督框架，从RGB-D观测中学习联合视觉-空间表示。该方法通过_patch间和_patch内融合将深度衍生的几何先验与视觉主干网络集成，在保持语义迁移能力的同时，显著提升了3D几何理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型主要在RGB图像上训练，但许多具身系统具备深度传感能力，可提供单目输入无法恢复的几何信息。现有方法未能充分利用深度信息与视觉特征的融合。

Method: 提出一种跨_patch融合和_patch内融合机制，将深度衍生的几何先验与视觉主干网络整合，使模型能够有效编码外观和空间结构特征。

Result: 在多个3D几何基准测试中超越同规模先验方法，同时在RGB-D语义分割任务中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了深度信息与视觉信息的联合表示学习，在3D感知和语义分割任务上均展现出优异性能，为具身智能系统的视觉理解提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce a self-supervised framework for learning joint visuospatial representations from RGB-D observations. While modern vision foundation models are trained almost exclusively on RGB images, many embodied systems have access to explicit depth sensing, which provides geometric information that monocular inputs cannot recover. Our method integrates depth-derived geometric priors with a visual backbone through inter-patch and intra-patch fusion, enabling the model to encode both appearance and spatial structure efficiently. The resulting representation shows promising improvements on 3D awareness while preserving semantic transfer: it outperforms prior methods of comparable scale on multiple 3D geometry benchmarks, and remains competitive when probed for standard RGB-D semantic segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [82] [UniFLM: United Segmentation and Measurement on Fetal Limb Ultrasonic Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27240)
*Zeen Zhou,Qiuhua Chen,Xiaojun Cao,Changmao Chen,Chao Sun,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniFLM统一框架，用于胎儿四肢长骨的跨平面分割和测量，解决超声图像中的噪声和语义差距问题，并构建了高质量的FLB数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型忽略胎儿致死性骨骼发育不良，且缺乏高质量标注数据和多长骨统一框架，通用分割模型难以处理超声图像的固有噪声和语义差距。

Method: 构建了包含肱骨、股骨、胫腓骨、桡尺骨高质量标注的FLB数据集；提出UniFLM统一框架，包含语义感知跳跃连接模块、正样本采样策略和点回归映射模块。

Result: 在FLB数据集上的广泛实验表明，UniFLM在胎儿长骨评估方面达到最优精度和增强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFLM框架能够有效解决超声图像分割中的噪声和语义差距问题，为胎儿骨骼发育不良的产前诊断提供有力工具。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound examination is crucial for assessing fetal limb development and detecting congenital anomalies. However, existing artificial intelligence models often overlook fetal lethal skeletal dysplasias due to the lack of high-quality annotated data and a unified framework for multiple long bones. Moreover, generic segmentation models struggle with the inherent noise and semantic gaps in ultrasound images. To address these challenges, we construct the Fetal Limb Bones (FLB) dataset, comprising high-quality annotations for the humerus, femur, tibia-fibula, and radius-ulna. Furthermore, we propose UniFLM, a unified framework for automatic cross-plane segmentation and measurement. UniFLM incorporates a Semantic-Aware Skip Connection module to bridge the semantic gap between encoder and decoder features, and a Positive Sampling strategy to adaptively filter noise and extract essential semantic information. Finally, a Point Regression Mapping module is introduced to learn clinician annotation patterns for precise bone length measurement. Extensive experiments conducted on the FLB dataset demonstrate that the proposed UniFLM achieves superior accuracy and enhanced generalization capabilities in fetal long bone assessment compared to current state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [83] [Sidecar: Training-Free Semantic Reuse for Character-Consistent Free-form Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27280)
*Sibo Dong,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sidecar是一个即插即用的语义增强模块，用于解决自由形式故事生成中的人物身份一致性问题。它从初始描述中保留实体级别信息，并将其注入到后续提示嵌入中，无需额外训练或修改基础扩散模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由形式故事生成中，人物在首次介绍时才有完整描述，之后仅通过类型级提及或代词引用。这导致后续提示缺少重要的身份相关语义，使角色一致性难以维持。

Method: 提出Sidecar模块，从初始描述中提取并保存实体级别信息，在后续帧生成时将缺失的语义注入提示嵌入中。该模块为即插即用设计，无需额外训练，也不修改基础扩散模型架构。

Result: 在FreeStoryBench上的实验表明，Sidecar在SDXL和FLUX多个基线模型上均能持续提升提示-图像对齐和角色一致性，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Sidecar为解决视觉故事生成中的角色一致性提供了一种有效且轻量级的解决方案，具有良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Visual storytelling requires generating images that follow a narrative while preserving consistent character identities across frames. In free-form story generation, a character is fully described only when first introduced and is later referred to by a type-level mention or pronoun. Although this setting better reflects natural storytelling, later prompts may omit important identity-related semantics, making character consistency more difficult to maintain. We propose \textbf{{Sidecar}}, a plug-and-play semantic augmentation module that preserves entity-level information from the initial description and injects the missing semantics into later prompt embeddings. Sidecar requires no additional training and does not modify the architecture of the base diffusion model. Experiments on FreeStoryBench show that Sidecar consistently improves prompt-image alignment and character consistency across multiple SDXL- and FLUX-based baselines, with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [84] [TADP: Task-Aware Deformable Prediction for Single-Stage 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27282)
*Su Wang,Yaochen Li,Min Yang,Jiaohao Nie,Chang Liu,Yuehu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务感知可变形预测（TADP）方法，用于单阶段3D目标检测，解决传统方法中不同任务共享相同特征的问题。该方法通过三重重特征细化聚合模块、多尺度特征聚合块和可变形预测头来提升检测性能，在KITTI数据集上达到80.91%的汽车mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段3D目标检测器使用相同的提取特征完成不同任务，但将特征投影到适合所有任务的通用空间是不可能的。因此需要设计一种任务自适应的方法来处理这一问题。

Method: 1. 三重重特征细化聚合模块：自适应提取三层次特征；2. 多尺度特征聚合块：以尺度感知的方式融合多尺度特征；3. 可变形预测头：即插即用模块，可感知每个任务的强调和交互；4. 设计了三种不同的可变形模块。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上的实验表明，汽车mAP达到80.91%，超越了KITTI基准上的许多最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所设计的可变形预测头在其它检测方法上也表现出良好的效果，验证了方法的泛化能力和有效性。

Abstract: Most single-stage 3D object detectors complete different tasks with the same extracted features. Nevertheless, it is impossible to project features into a common space that is adaptive for all the tasks. We present a novel task-aware deformable prediction (TADP) method for single-stage 3D object detection to solve this problem. Firstly, a triple feature refinement aggregation module is designed to extract three-level features adaptively. Additionally, we design the multi-scale feature aggregation block to fuse multi-scale features in a scale-aware manner. Finally, the prediction of each task is deformed with the designed plug-and-play task-aware deformation head. It can percept the emphasis and interaction of each task. We also designed three different deformation modules. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed deformation head shows good results on other detection methods. The experimental results on the KITTI dataset demonstrate that the car mAP is 80.91%, surpassing many state-of-the-art methods on the KITTI benchmark.

</details>


### [85] [Detection of Christmas tree plantations from high-resolution aerial imagery. A case study in the French Morvan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27290)
*Francesca Razzano,Emanuele Dalsasso,Adrien Baysse-Lainé,Silvia Liberata Ullo,Gilda Schirinzi,Jocelyn Chanussot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将圣诞树种植园的遥感检测定义为稀有目标语义分割问题，提出硬负样本挖掘(HNM)策略提升模型判别能力。采用DeepLabV3+ResNet-34架构，在2020年测试集上实现IoU=0.733、F1=0.846，HNM使PR-AUC从0.204提升至0.913。时间迁移性良好，2017/2018和2023年均可实现有效检测，但大规模验证表明该任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圣诞树种植园是经济上重要的遥感应用领域，但面临诸多挑战：高种植密度、短轮伐期、与周围植被的视觉混淆、仅一年参考年的密集标签、严重的类别不平衡。现有深度学习方法通常针对森林、普通种植系统或果园，不专门针对圣诞树种植园的特殊结构特征和困难负样本混淆问题。

Method: (i)将圣诞树种植园映射框架化为独特的稀有目标语义分割问题；(ii)引入硬负样本挖掘(HNM)策略来提高对混淆背景模式的判别能力；(iii)在多个层面评估框架：监督测试、时间迁移和大规模验证。使用DeepLabV3+ResNet-34编码器作为最佳模型架构。

Result: 在2020年测试集上，DeepLabV3+ResNet-34达到IoU=0.733，F1=0.846。HNM显著改善精确率-召回率行为，PR曲线下面积从0.204提高到0.913。时间迁移性良好：2017/2018年IoU/F1为0.751/0.858，2023年为0.691/0.817。大规模验证显示圣诞树种植园仅占很小比例：2017/2018年1498.4公顷(1.72%)，2023年1782.2公顷(2.04%)，总面积87309.4公顷。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将圣诞树种植园映射确立为遥感中的稀有目标语义分割问题，HNM策略有效提升了模型对混淆背景的判别能力，显著改善了精确率-召回率性能。模型展现出良好的时间迁移能力，但大规模验证揭示了该任务仍具挑战性，需要进一步研究改进。

Abstract: Christmas tree plantations are economically relevant, yet a largely unexplored application domain in Remote Sensing (RS). Their delineation is challenging because of high planting density, short rotation cycles, visual confusion with surrounding vegetation, the availability of dense labels for one reference year only, and severe class imbalance at the landscape scale. Although Deep Learning (DL) methods have shown strong potential for vegetation mapping, existing approaches are typically designed for forests, generic plantation systems, or orchards, and do not explicitly address the structural specificity and hard-negative confusion that characterize Christmas tree plantations. In response to these challenges, this work makes three main contributions: (i) it frames Christmas tree plantation mapping as a distinct rare-target semantic segmentation problem; (ii) it introduces a Hard Negative Mining (HNM) strategy to improve discrimination against confusing background patterns; and (iii) it evaluates the proposed framework across complementary levels, including supervised testing, temporal transfer, and large-scale validation. On the 2020 test set held out, the best model, DeepLabV3 with a ResNet-34 encoder, achieves an IoU of 0.733 and an F1-score of 0.846. HNM substantially improves precision-recall behavior, increasing the area under the precision-recall curve from 0.204 to 0.913. Temporal inference further shows meaningful transferability, reaching IoU/F1 values of 0.751/0.858 on 2017/2018 and 0.691/0.817 on 2023. Large-scale validation further highlights the intrinsic difficulty of the task, as Christmas tree plantations occupied only a very small fraction of the extent of the common evaluation, corresponding to 1,498.4 ha (1.72\%) in 2017/2018 and 1,782.2 ha (2.04\%) in 2023 out of 87,309.4 ha in total.

</details>


### [86] [R2M-Bench: Evaluating Revisit Memory via Relative Consistency in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27328)
*Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Rui Chen,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qishuai Wen,Li Niu,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R2M-Bench是一个相对重访记忆基准，通过将重访对与对照组进行比较来解决视频世界模型中重访相似性的歧义问题，提出了MemoryGain和Normalized Memory Ratio两个新指标来区分特定重访一致性和通用时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视频世界模型中绝对重访分数对渲染稳定性、重复内容和运动失败敏感的问题，因为首次访问与返回帧的高相似性可能只是因为中间 rollout 变化很小，而非模型真正记住了场景。

Method: R2M-Bench为每个检测到的返回比较三类帧对：1)重访对(first-visit和return)；2)间隙匹配的非重访对(测量通用时间稳定性)；3)短程对(估计短视野一致性)。由此计算MemoryGain(MG)和Normalized Memory Ratio(NMR)指标。数据集包含100个参考场景和3条离开-返回轨迹共300个实例，评估外观保真度、场景和对象身份、局部几何和持久状态四个维度。

Result: 在7个动作条件视频世界模型中，Overall NMR与人类一致性判断的Spearman相关系数为ρ=0.547(95% CI [0.45,0.63])。其与生成运动的模型内相关为0.072，而原始重访相似性为0.207，表明相对校准 substantially减少了慢动作捷径。DreamX-World-Memo获得最高Overall NMR。

Conclusion: 同 rollout 相对校准是区分重访特定一致性与通用时间稳定性的实用方法，为评估视频世界模型的记忆能力提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: High similarity between first-visit and return frames does not necessarily show that a video world model remembered the scene; the intervening rollout may simply have changed very little. This ambiguity makes absolute revisit scores sensitive to rendering stability, repetitive content, and failed motion. We introduce \emph{{R2M-Bench}} (\textbf{{R}}elative \textbf{{R}}evisit \textbf{{M}}emory Benchmark), a benchmark of observable revisit-selective consistency. For every detected return, R2M-Bench compares the revisit pair with two controls from the same rollout: a gap-matched non-revisit pair that measures generic temporal stability and a short-range pair that estimates short-horizon consistency. These comparisons produce \emph{{MemoryGain}} (MG), the revisit advantage over the temporal baseline, and the \emph{{Normalized Memory Ratio}} (NMR), which normalizes this advantage by the short-to-baseline dynamic range. R2M-Bench combines 100 reference scenes with three leave-and-return trajectories to form 300 instances and evaluates appearance fidelity, scene and object identity, local geometry, and persistent state. Across seven action-conditioned video world models, Overall NMR correlates with human consistency judgments at Spearman's $ρ=0.547$ (95\% CI $[0.45,0.63]$). Its within-model correlation magnitude with generated motion is $0.072$, compared with $0.207$ for raw revisit similarity, indicating that relative calibration substantially reduces the slow-motion shortcut. DreamX-World-Memo achieves the highest Overall NMR among the evaluated video models. Together, these results support same-rollout relative calibration as a practical way to distinguish revisit-specific consistency from generic temporal stability.

</details>


### [87] [PAWBench: How Far Are We from Probabilistically Aligned World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27345)
*Yuandong Pu,Le Zhuo,Sayak Paul,Gabriel Jorge Menezes,Avram Đorđević,Shiyang Li,Yifan Zhou,Bin Fu,Wenlong Zhang,Junjun He,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jingbo Xing,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率对齐(Probabilistic Alignment)的概念，用于评估视频生成模型作为世界模型的质量。研究者创建了PAWBench基准和PAWEval评估协议，发现现有11个模型在50个场景中均无法同时匹配参考概率并恢复有效行为范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评估主要关注单条视频的合理性，但忽略了世界模型应具备的分布级能力——即在相同初始观察和动作下再现可能行为的正确分布。研究者希望回答当前视频生成器与概率对齐世界建模之间存在多大差距。

Method: 1) 形式化概率对齐作为世界模型的分布准则；2) 构建PAWBench基准评估视频生成器作为世界动力学随机采样器；3) 开发PAWEval协议将重复视频 rollout 转换为物理行为的经验分布；4) 在50个场景和11个当前系统上测试。

Result: 实验表明：所有模型均无法在保持行为范围覆盖的同时一致地匹配参考概率。即便是当前最先进的系统，在概率对齐方面都存在显著差距。此外，语言提示、初始噪声采样或模型训练均难以有效重塑模型的预测分布。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成器与概率对齐的世界建模目标之间存在显著差距。PAWBench和PAWEval可为未来概率对齐世界建模的努力提供基础和评估框架。

Abstract: Recent video generation models are increasingly framed as world models. Many physical processes can unfold in more than one valid way. Therefore, a world model should reproduce not only a plausible trajectory, but also the distribution of possible behaviors under the same initial observation and action. We call this distribution-level requirement probabilistic alignment. However, existing evaluations largely assess individual-video plausibility and do not test whether repeated generations recover the correct distribution. This raises a central question: how far are current video generators from probabilistically aligned world modeling? To answer it, we formalize probabilistic alignment as a distributional criterion for world models and introduce PAWBench, a benchmark for evaluating video generators as stochastic samplers of world dynamics. We further introduce PAWEval, an outcome-level protocol that converts repeated video rollouts into empirical distributions over possible physical behaviors. Across 50 scenarios and eleven current systems, no model consistently matches the reference probabilities while recovering the range of valid behaviors. Having established this gap, we test whether language prompts, initial noise sampling, or model training can reshape the model's predictive distribution. We believe our work can serve as a foundation for future efforts to move towards probabilistically aligned world modeling.

</details>


### [88] [KnockGS:interaction-Grounded Calibrationof Physical Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27365)
*Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Bowen Jing,Jiyuan Cai,Xiaofeng Wang,Hongsen Lei,Weitao Zhou,Dandan Zhang,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KnockGS是一个物理集成的3D Gaussian表示框架，能够从物体在已知外力下的动力学响应中估计材料的弹性模量和密度比例，实现物理校准的3D Gaussian资产重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D Gaussian重建管道假设材料参数已知或手动指定，限制了其在需要从观察到的物体动态推断参数场景中的应用。

Method: 将力诱导响应作为校准信号：从观察到的动态中提取时间响应特征，估计两个材料尺度（弹性和密度），然后将估计值冻结并写回模拟器进行测试。

Result: 在5个保留材料目标上，该方法比响应检索、全局回归或固定默认材料更准确地恢复尺度；估计值对方向和幅度不同的交互保持预测性。

Conclusion: 交互响应携带足够信息来校准物理嵌入的3D Gaussian表示中的材料尺度，是实现渲染外观与模拟响应一致的交互式PhysicalGS系统的第一步。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations now allow reconstructed deformable objects to be simulated and rendered under explicit material models. Existing pipelines, however, assume that material parameters are known or manually specified, limiting their applicability when these parameters must be inferred from observed object dynamics. We propose KnockGS, an interaction-response PhysicalGS framework that estimates the elasticity and density scales of a 3D Gaussian object from its dynamics under a known applied force. Rather than treating physical simulation only as a forward process, we turn the force-induced response into a calibration signal: temporal response features are xtracted from the observed dynamics, the two material scales are estimated from those features, and the estimate is then frozen and written back into the same simulator so that it can be tested on an interaction it was never fitted to.We evaluate the framework on both parameter recovery and response-level fidelity. The estimated scales are compared against hidden ground truth, and the re-simulated object is measured against the target using 3D particle trajectories, response-curve statistics, and rendered-frame quality. Across five held-out material targets, our method recovers the scales substantially more accurately than response retrieval, global regression, or a fixed default material, and the frozen estimate remains predictive under interactions that differ in direction and in magnitude. Interaction response therefore carries enough information to calibrate material scales in physically grounded 3D Gaussian representations.Our study is a first step toward interactive PhysicalGS systems that calibrate a Gaussian asset whose rendered appearance and simulated response are consistent.

</details>


### [89] [Successive Capacity Growth: Task-Complexity-Driven Width and Depth Expansion for Vision Transformer Encoders in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27367)
*Frederik Berenz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出成功容量增长(SCG)方法，用于JEPAs世界模型中Vision Transformer编码器的动态扩展。SCG从最小的编码器开始，根据任务需求逐步扩展宽度或深度，无需预先分配最大容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小的Vision Transformer编码器存在容量分配不当问题：简单任务时过度配置，复杂任务时配置不足，且注意力头存在显著冗余。需要一种任务自适应的容量分配方法。

Method: SCG从最小编码器(1个注意力头、2层、283K参数)开始，通过宽度扩展(添加注意力头增强低层语义容量)或深度扩展(添加Transformer块增强高层语义抽象)逐步增长。引入SIGReg(草图各向同性高斯正则器)确保语义维度统计独立且与预测目标对齐，防止表示崩溃。采用函数保持扩展机制安全地试用架构变更，若不改善预测损失则回滚。

Result: 在60维多目标动力学任务中，SCG触发深度扩展，预测损失比固定小型基线改善20.3%，参数效率比固定大型模型高56倍；在2D导航任务中，单次宽度扩展即比固定大型模型改善23%。在三个递增复杂度的环境中，自适应编码器均匹配或超越固定小型基线，零误报扩展，函数精确保持(比率=1.0，绝对差异=0.0)。

Conclusion: JEPA世界模型编码器无需预先分配最大容量，可随任务需求逐步增长，在保持表示质量的同时实现显著的计算和数据效率。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling typically employ fixed-size Vision Transformer encoders that are over-provisioned for simple tasks and under-provisioned for complex ones, with significant redundancy across attention heads. We propose Successive Capacity Growth (SCG), a method that starts from a minimal encoder (1 head, 2 layers, 283K parameters) and grows incrementally in width (adding attention heads for low-level semantic capacity) or depth (adding transformer blocks for higher-order semantic abstraction), driven by a task-agnostic test-and-verify mechanism that exploits function-preserving expansion to safely trial architectural changes and roll back if they do not improve prediction loss. The Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) ensures that all learned semantic dimensions remain statistically independent and aligned with the predictive objective, preventing collapse even as the architecture grows. On a 60-dimensional multi-object dynamics task, SCG naturally triggers depth expansion, improving prediction loss by 20.3% over the fixed small baseline with 56 times greater parameter efficiency than scaling to the fixed large model; on a 2D navigation task, a single width expansion yields even an 23% improvement over the fixed large model. Across all three tested environments of increasing complexity, the adaptive encoder matches or exceeds the fixed small baseline, with zero false-positive expansions and bit-exact function preservation (ratio = 1.0, absolute difference = 0.0). The take-away is that JEPA world model encoders need not be pre-allocated at maximum capacity - they can grow successively as the task demands, achieving significant compute and data efficiency while maintaining representation quality.

</details>


### [90] [LeVJEPA: Efficient & Scalable Video Pretraining without the Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27395)
*Lukas Kuhn,Lucas Maes,Giuseppe Serra,Quentin Le Lidec,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeVJEPA是首个基于LeJEPA无崩溃目标训练的视频编码器，通过单一编码器+投影器架构和SIGReg正则化避免表示崩溃，在计算效率上比V-JEPA 2低5.6-20.8倍，且支持块因果注意力训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频学习表示在计算上仍然昂贵，现有自监督方法需要非对称架构、指数移动平均目标编码器、停止梯度操作和容量受限的预测器来防止表示崩溃，或者通过重建像素空间来规避崩溃问题。

Method: 使用单一编码器架构，通过全局和局部视图的对比损失训练，配合SIGReg正则化排除崩溃。架构简化为编码器和投影器，目标函数简化为单个超参数。支持统一随机token丢弃降低计算成本，可使用块因果注意力训练。

Result: 在相同数据相同epoch条件下，LeVJEPA与V-JEPA 2相当或超越，ViT-S/B/L规模下计算量减少5.6-20.8倍；在匹配FLOPs下比最强视频基线高7.6个ImageNet-1K点；与计算匹配的DINOv2相比，在外观中心评估接近图像预训练编码器，运动中心准确率几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 视频可能成为通用视觉预训练的可行且在多方面更优的基材，一旦移除其计算开销；块因果注意力使时间顺序成为编码器本身的特性，无需分支间非对称性。

Abstract: Video carries the temporal structure of the physical world, yet learning representations from it has remained computationally expensive: prevailing self-supervised methods either prevent representation collapse through architectural asymmetries, coupling an exponential-moving-average target encoder, a stop-gradient, and a capacity-limited predictor, or circumvent it by reconstructing masked content in pixel space. We introduce LeVJEPA, the first video encoder trained under LeJEPA's collapse-free objective, which dispenses with both. A single encoder is trained with an invariance loss over global and local views of a clip, regularized by SIGReg, which excludes collapse with a provable guarantee. The architecture reduces to an encoder and a projector, and the objective to a single hyperparameter. This formulation admits two properties. First, the cost of pretraining is governed by the number of tokens the encoder observes; uniform random token dropping renders this number small while simultaneously improving downstream accuracy. At matched epochs on identical data, LeVJEPA matches or surpasses V-JEPA 2 across ViT-S/B/L at 5.6 to 20.8x less pretraining compute, and at matched total FLOPs it exceeds the strongest video baseline by 7.6 points on ImageNet-1K while remaining competitive on motion-centric benchmarks. Second, since no asymmetry between branches is required, the encoder can be trained with block-causal attention at no measurable accuracy cost: temporal ordering becomes a property of the encoder itself. Against a compute-matched DINOv2 trained on frames of the same videos, LeVJEPA approaches the image-pretrained encoder on appearance-centric evaluation while nearly doubling its motion-centric accuracy. These results indicate that, once its computational overhead is removed, video becomes a viable and in several respects preferable substrate for general-purpose visual pretraining.

</details>


### [91] [Reconstructing Humans and Objects in Interaction using Large Reconstruction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27407)
*Agniv Chatterjee,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MILO是一个利用大重建模型(LRM)的视觉能力从单张图像恢复详细3D人-物交互的框架。通过利用LRM提供的强大几何骨架，将问题重新定义为解释LRM网格：将其分割成人物和物体组件，将参数化人体模型拟合到人体部分，并可选地将物体模板对齐到物体部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人-物交互重建是3D计算机视觉中的基础问题，应用于AR/VR、机器人和具身AI。但由于深度歧义、遮挡和物体形状变化，重建仍然具有挑战性。现有方法主要关注重投影和接触约束，将参数化人体模型和物体模板拟合到2D图像。

Method: 提出MILO框架，利用大重建模型(LRM)的视觉能力。核心观点是LRM提供了强大的几何骨架，保留了相对人-物排列和接近度线索。将重建问题重新定义为解释LRM网格：分割成人和物体组件，拟合参数化人体模型到人体部分，可选地对齐物体模板到物体部分。

Result: MILO在多个基准和交互场景中实现了强大的重建精度，并优于现有基线。

Conclusion: MILO通过利用LRM的几何 scaffold 简化了3D HOI重建流程，为从单张图像恢复详细3D人-物交互提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Estimation of Human-Object Interactions in 3D (3D HOI) is a fundamental problem in 3D computer vision with applications in AR/VR, robotics, and embodied AI. However, reconstructing these interactions in 3D remains challenging due to depth ambiguities, occlusions, and object shape variability. Existing approaches are primarily concerned with reprojection and contact constraints, fitting parametric human models and object templates to 2D images. In this paper, we explore a different avenue. We present MILO, a framework that leverages the visual capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs) to recover detailed 3D human-object interactions from a single image. Our key observation is that LRMs provide a powerful geometric scaffold that preserves relative human-object arrangement and proximity cues. This significantly simplifies the reconstruction procedure, reframing the problem as interpreting the LRM mesh: we segment it into human and object components, fit a parametric body model to the human part, and optionally align an object template to the object part (if such a template is available). MILO achieves strong reconstruction accuracy and outperforms existing baselines across multiple benchmarks and interaction scenarios. Our code is available at https://ac5113.github.io/MILO.

</details>


### [92] [Retrieval Heads Meet Vision: Uncovering How VLMs Locate and Extract Visual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27417)
*Chanho Park,Daehyeon Choi,Jihyun Lee,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现了视觉语言模型(VLM)中存在视觉检索头(VRH)的机制,这是约1.7-2.6%的注意力头,负责将文本描述 grounding 到图像区域。通过掩码实验证明,仅掩码前20个VRH即可使定位准确率下降高达80个百分点,而掩码相同数量的随机头则影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借鉴大语言模型中的检索头机制,探索视觉语言模型是否具有类似的视觉检索机制,以理解和解释VLM如何将文本描述与图像区域进行对应。

Method: 提出统一设计空间整合现有头部评分方法,基于查询 token、key聚合和跨样本聚合进行评分。使用输出预测token对真实引用区域进行求和来识别因果注意力头。

Result: 在11个VLM和5个指代表达基准测试中,仅掩码约20个VRH可使定位准确率下降高达80个百分点。VRH具有跨任务泛化能力,可迁移到不同视觉编码器、投影器和指令微调的VLM中。

Conclusion: VLM中存在类似LLM文本检索头的视觉检索头机制,这些头在功能上特异,在架构上可共享,为理解和改进VLM的可解释性和可控性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can locate an image region referred to by a text prompt and route the corresponding visual evidence to the output, yet the internal mechanism behind this behavior is not understood. Inspired by retrieval heads in large language models, we ask whether VLMs contain an analogous mechanism for visual retrieval. We answer affirmatively by introducing Visual Retrieval Heads (VRHs), a small subset of attention heads (about 1.7-2.6%) that are causally responsible for grounding text descriptions to image regions. To find them, we recast existing head-scoring methods under a unified design space over query tokens, key aggregation, and cross-sample aggregation. We then show that scoring attention from output prediction tokens with a sum over the ground-truth referent region most reliably identifies causal heads. Across eleven VLMs and five referring-expression benchmarks, masking only the top 20 VRHs reduces grounding accuracy by up to 80 percentage points, while masking the same number of random heads has little effect. Beyond replicating the causal-sparse-universal triad established for text retrieval heads, VRHs exhibit several properties not previously reported: they generalize across visual reference tasks, remaining causal on attribute, spatial, counting, and visual-math benchmarks despite being discovered through bounding-box prediction; they are functionally specific, preserving output format while corrupting localization; and they are architecturally shared, transferring causally across VLMs that share an LLM backbone but differ in vision encoder, projector, and instruction tuning.

</details>


### [93] [UrbanGround: From Local Perception to Spatial Agency in a Real-Scale City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27456)
*Tianjie Ju,Zheng Wu,Yueqing Sun,Yuhan Cui,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Pengzhou Cheng,Haodong Zhao,Zongru Wu,Xinbei Ma,Doris Zhang,Kunling Li,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu,Hao Fei,Qi Gu,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UrbanGround沙盒环境，测试当前多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)代理能否将本地城市感知转化为复杂真实城市环境中的可靠行动。研究发现MLLMs在视觉识别和短程空间推理方面表现良好，但方向感知和行人意识运动不可靠，主要失败在于扩展探索时本地能力无法组合成持续的目标导向行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市代理取决于本地证据在代理移动后是否仍然有用。现有MLLMs可以解释街景，但能否将这种本地感知转化为可靠的行动仍是未知数。需要一个物理约束的真实城市副本来测试这一能力。

Method: 构建UrbanGround沙盒环境，基于香港全域3D地理空间数据创建物理约束的城市副本，支持第一人称视角的闭环交互和导航交互地图。通过三个研究问题逐步测试：1)局部场景理解和空间问答；2)远距离导航支持；3)应对路线变化和行人运动的行为适应。

Result: 当代MLLMs代理在视觉识别和短程空间推理方面展现有用的原子能力，但方向感和行人感知运动仍不可靠。核心失败出现在扩展探索过程中：本地能力无法组合成持续的目标导向行为，错误累积且缺乏有效纠正。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs代理在复杂开放城市环境中的可靠探索能力有限。UrbanGround为研究MLLMs在城市环境中的导航和探索能力提供了重要基准测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can interpret a street view, but urban agency depends on whether such local evidence remains useful after the agent starts to move. In this paper, we investigate how far current MLLM agents can turn local urban perception into reliable action in a complicated real-scale city. We propose UrbanGround, the first sandbox to make this question testable in a physically constrained replica of Hong Kong built from territory-wide 3D geospatial data. UrbanGround supports closed-loop interaction from a first-person view and provides an interactive map for navigation. Agents can directly enter the 3D city and explore from a first-person view. Our analysis follows the growth of the spatial problem through three research questions. We first test whether an agent can ground a local scene well enough to answer spatial questions after active observation. Then we ask whether that grounding supports navigation as destinations become farther away and less explicit. Finally, we examine whether the resulting behavior survives changes in route availability and pedestrian motion. Contemporary MLLM agents usually show useful atomic abilities in visual recognition and short-range spatial reasoning, while orientation and pedestrian-aware movement remain unreliable. Their central failure emerges over extended exploration, where local abilities do not compose into sustained goal-directed behavior and errors accumulate without effective correction. We hope UrbanGround will support broader study of how far current MLLM agents can explore reliably in complex, open-ended urban environments.

</details>

### [94] [Quadratic Complexity of Voronoi Diagrams in $\mathbb{{R}}^3$ for Lines in a Single Ruling of a Regulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27114)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究三维空间中一条光滑双直纹二次曲面上一个 ruling 上的 n 条直线的最近和最远 Voronoi 图。在一般位置假设下，证明两种图的顶点数最多为 4n(n-3)，总组合复杂度为 O(n^2)。对于最近 Voronoi 图，存在达到 (n-2)(n-3)/2 个正则顶点的构造，证明最坏情况下复杂度为 Θ(n^2)。最远 Voronoi 图在任何一般位置输入下复杂度均为 Θ(n^2)。此外给出 O(n^2) 时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景：一般位置下任意线集合的最近 Voronoi 图复杂度仅知介于 Ω(n^2) 和 O(n^{{3+ε}}) 之间问题：特殊类别（单 ruling 上的直线）能否降低或确定复杂度界限？

Method: 使用 Plücker 嵌入将直线映射到 Plücker 空间，ruling 对应一条二次曲线；直线与球面相切条件化为四次多项式；利用 Bézout 定理估计各类中心的数量；通过循环排序参数实现 O(n^2) 时间算法

Result: 最近和最远 Voronoi 图顶点数上界为 4n(n-3)，总复杂度 O(n^2)；存在至少 (n-2)(n-3)/2 个正则顶点的构造；最远图复杂度始终为 Θ(n^2)

Conclusion: 在双直纹二次面的单 ruling 上，直线集合的 Voronoi 图复杂度退化为 Θ(n^2)，相比一般位置的三维线集有所改进，并给出精确的 O(n^2) 时间算法

Abstract: We study nearest and farthest Voronoi diagrams of lines in $\mathbb{{R}}^3$ under the Euclidean metric when all $n$ lines belong to one ruling of a smooth doubly ruled real quadric. For arbitrary line sites, the combinatorial complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram is known only to lie between $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^{{3+\varepsilon}})$. Under general-position assumptions, we prove that both diagrams in the ruling class have at most $4n(n-3)$ vertices and $O(n^2)$ total combinatorial complexity. Conversely, for every $n \ge 4$, one ruling of a fixed non-rotational one-sheeted hyperboloid contains a general-position set of $n$ lines with at least $(n-2)(n-3)/2$ distinct regular nearest vertices, where regular means that exactly four lines support the vertex and their three defining bisectors meet transversely. Thus the worst-case complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram in this class is $Θ(n^2)$, while the farthest diagram has $Θ(n^2)$ complexity for every general-position input, since it has exactly $n(n-1)$ three-dimensional cells. Under the Plücker embedding, the ruling is a conic, and the condition for a line to be tangent to a Euclidean sphere restricts to a binary quartic. At a regular vertex, the four supporting parameters exhaust its roots, and sign alternation forces two arcs of the parameter circle to be site-free. This leaves only $n(n-3)/2$ possible cyclic support types, while Bézout's theorem bounds the number of centers for each type by eight. The same reduction yields an exact $O(n^2)$-time algorithm that, after cyclically sorting the site parameters, enumerates all finite nearest and farthest vertices as constant-degree real univariate representations.

</details>


### [95] [Lunar Generalizations of the Euclidean Minimum Spanning Tree in the Plane and their Expected Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27118)
*Ondřej Draganov,Herbert Edelsbrunner,Sophie Rosenmeier,Morteza Saghafian*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 将欧几里得最小生成树(EMST)推广到多色点情形，提出月球EMST概念，证明随机彩色点的期望成本收敛到常数乘以√n，但常数精确值未知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 chromatic persistent homology 引入的启发，将EMST从单色点推广到多彩色点情形，构建更一般的拓扑结构。

Method: 引入 lune（不同颜色点为中心、半径为r的s+1个圆的交集）概念，跟随r从0到∞过程中树结构的变化，定义成本为弧和节点形成时半径差的两倍。

Result: 当点在[0,1]^2中均匀随机分布且随机着色时，期望成本随n趋于无穷大收敛到某个常数（仅依赖s）乘以√n。

Conclusion: 证明了该常数存在，这是主要贡献；但与经典EMST类似，常数的精确值仍未得到。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent introduction of chromatic persistent homology, we generalize the Euclidean minimum spanning tree (EMST) for $n$ points in $\mathbb{{R}}^2$ to the lunar EMST for the case in which the points come in $s+1$ colors. Calling the intersection of $s+1$ disks of radius $r$ centered at points with pairwise different colors a \emph{{lune}}, the generalized EMST reflects the history of the union of lunes as $r$ goes from $0$ to $\infty$, and its \emph{{cost}} is twice the difference between the radii when the arcs and nodes of the tree are formed. If the points are chosen uniformly at random in $[0,1]^2$ and colored randomly, the expected cost converges to some constant (that depends on $s$) times $\sqrt{{n}}$, as $n$ goes to infinity. The main contribution of this paper is a proof that this constant exists, however similar to the case of the classic EMST, its precise value remains elusive.

</details>

### [96] [Zero-Shot Self-Orchestration with Ledger-Based Control for Improved LLM Coding Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26480)
*Victor Gao,Vida Khosrowshahi,Ali Khosrowshahi,Xihao Sun,Juhyun Lee,Simon,Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究多智能体LLM系统相比单模型基线的效果，在100道高难度LiveCodeBench编程题上测试9种模型，发现管理器-工作者的脚手架架构能带来条件性收益：部分模型提升显著（Qwen3.8-27B提升23.4分，Kimi-K3提升30.4分），部分模型无提升甚至下降。管理器通过上下文管理和问题分解机制提升性能，但代价是token费用增加约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献报告多智能体LLM系统优于单模型基线，但证据混杂且比较往往混杂：管道同时改变token预算、工具调用和提示词，无法揭示真正有效的因素。研究旨在控制变量，单独评估管理器-工作者脚手架架构的效果。

Method: 在100道最新高难度LiveCodeBench编程题上，对9种模型（5种开源权重模型，4种前沿闭源模型）进行测试。比较使用管理器-工作者脚手架与单次通过同一模型的性能差异，进行5次配对测试，统计显著性检验。

Result: 收益因模型而异：Qwen3.8-27B提升23.4分，GPT-5.6-Luna提升10.6分，GPT-5.6-Terra提升8.0分，Kimi-K3提升30.4分，Minimax-M3提升11.0分（p<10^-4）；Qwen3.6-35B下降1-9分。Opus-5配合管理器达到91%最高分。管理器使token费用增加约3倍，但GPT-5.6-Terra配合管理器的准确率接近Fable 5单次调用（85.0 vs 87.4），价格仅为后者的1/5（$11.71 vs $61.11）。

Conclusion: 管理器脚手架的收益是条件性的，主要机制包括上下文管理和问题分解。对于大型推理模型收益较小；对于部分关闭推理的模型和启用推理的较小模型收益较大。该方法在不增加模型规模的情况下以较低成本提升准确率。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model systems are widely reported to beat single-model baselines, but the evidence is mixed, and comparisons are usually confounded: pipelines change token budgets, tool calls, and prompts simultaneously, so an aggregate gain rarely reveals what actually helped. We investigate the effect of introducing the manager-worker scaffold over a shared filesystem workspace, with no training and no per-benchmark tuning, measured against the same model answering in a single pass. Across nine models -- five open-weight, spanning 9B to ~2.8T parameters, and four frontier closed models -- on the 100 latest hard LiveCodeBench problems, the scaffold's benefit is real but conditional: large and statistically significant for some (Qwen3.8-27B +23.4, GPT-5.6-Luna +10.6 and GPT-5.6-Terra +8.0, each over five paired passes; Kimi-K3 +30.4 and Minimax-M3 +11.0 over five paired passes with reasoning off, both at $p < 10^{{-4}}$, and +42 and +12 in a single pass at a 128k cap) and null or negative for others (Qwen3.6-35B -1 to -9 with reasoning off). With the manager, Opus-5 achieves the highest score in the study at 91% in one pass. Running a manager roughly triples the token bill, but it buys accuracy more cheaply than moving to a larger model does: GPT-5.6-Terra with a manager nearly matches Fable 5's single-call accuracy (85.0 against 87.4, $p = 0.59$) at a fifth of the price (\$11.71 against \$61.11 per 100-problem pass, $p < 10^{{-4}}$), and the Qwen-27B arm does it for \$51.75 on weights anyone can self-host. Our transcript analysis finds several mechanisms behind the gains, of which two recur: context management, in which short worker calls and shared notes organize state and reduce truncation, and problem decomposition. Improvements are modest for large models with reasoning enabled, but larger for some models with reasoning disabled and for smaller models with reasoning enabled.

</details>


### [97] [Risks and Controls for Multi-Agent Systems: an analytical framework for deployment of AI agents across organisational boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26626)
*Alistair Reid,Simon O'Callaghan,Dustin Venini,Liam Carroll,Tiberio Caetano*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该报告提出了一个框架，帮助组织、政策制定者和研究者理解AI智能体交互时产生的风险，以及如何通过控制措施应对这些风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织部署AI智能体，这些智能体之间的交互将日益增加——在组织内部、与合作伙伴/客户/供应商的智能体、以及与互联网上未知交易对手的智能体。交互本身可能产生故障，且一旦跨组织边界，没有任何单一组织能完全监控、控制或治理这些交互。

Method: 报告引入了三个部署层级：单一治理（单一组织管理所有智能体）、联邦治理（多组织在共同规则下部署到共享环境）、开放环境（智能体无中央权威运营）。在每个层级中，检查风险因素、故障模式和可用控制措施。

Result: 识别出谁有能力施加控制，并确定无人能采取行动的差距所在，以及需要何种集体行动来弥补差距。

Conclusion: 报告为跨组织边界的AI智能体交互风险提供了系统性治理框架，涵盖从集中到分散的不同治理模式。)

Abstract: This report presents a framework to help organisations, policymakers and researchers reason about the risks that emerge when AI agents interact with each other, how those risks change as interactions cross organisational boundaries, and the controls that may help address them.
  As organisations deploy AI agents, those agents will increasingly interact with each other: inside the organisation, with the agents of partners, customers and suppliers, and with unknown counterparties on the open internet. Failures can emerge from the interactions themselves, and once those interactions cross an organisation's perimeter, no single organisation can fully see, control or govern them.
  The report introduces three deployment tiers, defined by the minimum common governance binding any two interacting agents: singular governance, where one organisation governs every agent; federated governance, where multiple organisations deploy into a shared environment under agreed rules; and open environments, where agents operate with no central authority and shared standards are adopted voluntarily if at all.
  Within each tier, the report examines risk factors, failure modes and available controls. It identifies who is positioned to apply the controls, and where no actor is positioned to act, it characterises the gap and the collective action required to close it.

</details>


### [98] [Fixed-Haven Reservation for Online Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Dense Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26759)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究固定避风港（Safe Haven）契约用于密集仓库中的在线多代理取货配送（MAPD）问题。每个代理拥有专属避风港，其他代理将其视为障碍物。理论证明该方法能完成所有有限发布的任务，实验表明SHARP在所有测试配置上达到100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集仓库布局（单通道、死巷、树状路径）缺乏传统MAPD所需的额外等待点或双连通拓扑，导致现有方法可能失效。需要一种不依赖这些假设的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出固定避风港契约：每个代理拥有专属安全避风港（通常为起点），仅所有者可占用，其他代理将其视为阻塞。实现SHARP系统，使繁忙或撤退代理沿预留无碰撞路径前往避风港。

Result: SHARP在所有测试配置上唯一达到100%成功率。树状布局中集中规划成本较高。固定返回机制是鲁棒性的核心。禁用中途重新分配使服务时间增加1.89倍，总完成时间增加1.53倍。

Conclusion: 固定避风港契约为密集仓库环境中的在线MAPD提供了鲁棒解决方案。避风港固定返回机制是树状布局鲁棒性的关键。保留撤退代理的路径预留对系统性能至关重要。

Abstract: Dense warehouses often contain single-lane aisles, dead ends, and tree-like guidepaths that leave little room for idle agents to wait without blocking others. Existing Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) guarantees for completing all finitely released tasks typically rely on extra waiting endpoints that planned paths can avoid, or on biconnected topology; these assumptions may fail in such layouts. We study fixed-Haven reservation for online MAPD, where pickup-delivery tasks are released over time. Each agent owns a fixed Safe Haven (Haven for short), usually its start cell, that only the owner may occupy and that other agents treat as blocked. For finite task releases, we prove that this fixed-Haven contract completes all released tasks under Haven-Reachability and explicit planning/progress assumptions. We implement the contract in SHARP, a Safe-Haven Retreat Planner that keeps every busy or retreating agent on a collision-free reserved route ending at its Haven. We compare SHARP with representative TP and PIBT-family MAPD baselines: Token Passing (TP), Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT), and PIBT with Temporary Priority and Temporary Avoidance (PIBTTP-TA) for biconnected main areas with attached trees. In the robustness sweep, SHARP is the only method with 100% success on all tested configurations, at substantially higher centralized planning cost on tree-like layouts. A TP-style fixed-home-return counterfactual with full-route validation also recovers robustness on tested tree-like layouts, suggesting that fixed return is a central robustness mechanism there. A no-overwrite variant shows that disabling mid-retreat reassignment worsens service time (release-to-delivery latency) by 1.89 times and makespan by 1.53 times in the tested high-load tree condition.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamic Haven Selection for Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Constrained Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26939)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: A-sharp是一种改进的多机器人取送任务规划系统，通过在任务分配时动态改变机器人的避难所目标，解决了原SHARP系统中固定避难所导致的路径迂回问题。该方法包含避难所可用性测试和待释放规则以防止冲突，并在理论上证明了不变性保持和有限释放完整性。实验表明A-sharp在72,000次运行中显著优于SHARP，在测试的树形地图上中位减少16.7%的最终交付时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP系统为每个机器人分配固定的初始避难所，但在完成配送任务后，机器人需要返回遥远的固定避难所，这会导致效率低下。特别是在空间受限的仓库布局中，单通道和死胡同工作站会加剧这一问题。

Method: 1) 在任务分配时动态选择避难所目标而非使用固定避难所；2) 提出避难所可用性测试，防止两个机器人使用同一等待位置；3) 提出待释放规则，在机器人离开前保持原避难所受保护；4) 在明确的避难所结构和安全区间路径规划(SIPP)假设下进行理论证明。

Result: 在14,400个地图-机器人数量-任务速率-随机种子组合上各运行72,000次。在138个避难所数量多于机器人数量的配置中，A-sharp在107个配置中显著优于SHARP，没有一个配置显著差于SHARP。在测试的树形地图上，中位makespan减少16.7%。

Conclusion: A-sharp通过动态避难所选择机制，在保持理论正确性（不变性保持和有限释放完整性）的同时，显著提高了多机器人取送任务在受限仓库布局中的效率，是一种更优的在线任务分配与路径规划解决方案。

Abstract: Space-efficient warehouse layouts often contain single-agent-width aisles and dead-end workstations where robots have few places to wait without blocking others. In Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) on such constrained layouts, robots must accept online pickup-delivery tasks while preserving protected waiting locations called Havens. The Safe HAven Retreat Planner (SHARP) introduced a mechanism that extends each committed task path with a validated retreat to the agent's dedicated initial Haven, but fixed-Haven commitments can send agents toward distant Havens after deliveries. We present A-sharp (Adaptive SHARP), which changes an agent's retreat target at task assignment time. A naive switch can cause two agents to rely on the same waiting location or let another committed path pass through a location that is still occupied or reserved. A-sharp prevents these failures with an availability test for candidate Havens and a pending-release rule that keeps the previous Haven protected until the agent departs. Under explicit Haven-structure and Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) assumptions, we prove invariant preservation and finite-release completeness: every task in any finite release sequence is delivered in finite time. Across 72,000 runs on 14,400 paired map-agent-count-rate-seed cases over four maps, both SHARP and A-sharp complete their respective 14,400 runs. For makespan (final delivery time), a prespecified paired comparison with Holm correction over all 138 configurations with more Havens than agents finds A-sharp significantly better in 107 configurations and never significantly worse than SHARP; on the tested tree map, the median reduction is 16.7%.

</details>


### [100] [One Model, Many Minds: Unlocking Multi-Agent Synergy in a Single Agent via Mixture of Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27338)
*Zhichen Zeng,Huiyuan Chen,Jingru Cheng,Juan Zha,Ming Liu,Ying Chen,Xiyuan Yang,Chaosheng Dong,Haiyang Zhang,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Mixture of Roles (MoRe)方法，解决LLM specialization中单一固定specialization无法适应多样化查询，以及多智能体系统(MAS)需要多轮交互导致推理成本高的问题。MoRe通过学习多样化的steering向量codebook，结合query-aware router动态融合多个角色到单一steering向量，实现单智能体单轮推理中的多视角专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM专业化方法存在局限：单智能体范式依赖预定义persona或steering向量，只能实现单一固定specialization，无法适应多样化查询；多智能体系统(MAS)虽能实现动态多视角问题解决，但融合specialization需要多轮交互，导致context长度和推理成本增加。需要一种能在单轮推理中自适应组合多个specialization的方法。

Method: 提出Mixture of Roles (MoRe)方法：1)学习多样化的steering向量codebook，每个向量编码一个潜在角色；2)query-aware router动态将codebook融合为包含多个角色的单一steering向量；3)使用融合后的向量引导backbone LLM实现多视角specialization。训练采用三阶段SFT课程和GRPO后训练，backbone LLM保持冻结状态。

Result: 在推理和人格基准测试中，MoRe相比单智能体基线平均提升2.2%，性能与MAS相当，同时token成本降低20倍。

Conclusion: MoRe成功实现了单智能体单轮推理中的多视角specialization，在保持与多智能体系统相当性能的同时显著降低了推理成本，为LLM专业化提供了一种高效灵活的新范式。

Abstract: Specializing Large Language Models (LLMs) toward distinct abilities underpins successes ranging from personalized assistants to multi-agent systems (MAS). Single-agent paradigms rely on pre-defined personas or steering vectors to induce specialization, yet they impose a single fixed specialization that fails to adapt to diverse queries. Conversely, MAS achieves dynamic multi-perspective problem solving by orchestrating agents with distinct text-based roles, but fusing these specializations requires multi-turn interactions that inflate context length and inference cost. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture of Roles (MoRe), which adaptively composes multiple specializations into a single steering vector for single-turn inference. Specifically, MoRe learns a diversified codeboox of steering vectors, each of which encodes a latent role. A query-aware router dynamically fuses the codebook into a steering vector that encompasses multiple roles. By steering the backbone LLM with the composed vector, MoRe enables multi-perspective specialization in a single-agent, single-turn inference process. The proposed MoRe can be efficiently trained via a three-stage SFT curriculum and GRPO post-training, while the backbone LLM remains frozen. Experiments across reasoning and personality benchmarks show that MoRe outperforms single-agent baselines by 2.2% on average, and achieves performance on par with MAS while reducing token cost by 20x.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EduRiskX是一个神经符号框架,结合时序Transformer预测器与F-Logic符号推理,用于预测在线教育中学生学业风险。该框架解决了现有模型早期检测能力有限和可解释性不足的问题,在OULAD数据集上准确率达0.900,F1分数为0.894,平均早期检测周为9.32周,检测率达94.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在预测学生学业风险时存在两大问题:1)早期检测能力有限,无法及时识别风险学生;2)可解释性不足,形成"黑箱"信任危机,阻碍了在实际教学环境中的应用。需要一种既能早期预警又可解释的预测方法。

Method: 提出EduRiskX神经符号框架,包含:1)神经组件使用时序注意力、类别加权损失和动态周截断来建模纵向学生活动序列;2)F-Logic规则库基于Engagement Theory和Student Integration Model教育理论构建,模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑;3)基于逻辑回归的融合机制结合神经风险概率和符号置信度分数。实验采用严格的80/10/10学生级划分。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上,EduRiskX在学期末(第38周)达到准确率0.900、F1分数0.894,平均早期检测周为9.32,检测率达94.30%。相比PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM、CNN等先进时序模型和深度学习基线,在相同条件下实现了更好的召回率和更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX通过神经符号融合方法有效提升了学业风险的早期检测能力和可解释性。F-Logic模块提供了将预测与可观察行为模式及教育理论相联系的结构化规则解释,有助于教育者理解决策原因。该框架在准确性和可解释性之间取得了良好平衡,为在线教育中的学生干预提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [102] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了ICU死亡风险预测中， standalone LLM与四步agentic pipeline在临床解释方面的差异。XGBoost模型AUROC达0.855，但重点是比较两种LLM解释方法的质量和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型虽能准确预测ICU死亡率，但特征归因方法缺乏临床叙事能力，难以满足床旁使用需求。需要LLM来弥补这一差距，并探索多步骤agentic管道的可行性。

Method: 使用eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院，死亡率8.1%），先训练XGBoost预测模型。然后在38例分层解释子集上比较standalone LLM与四步agentic pipeline的解释质量，评估指标包括：SHAP对齐度、方向一致性、指南 grounding、价值特异性、可信度等。

Result: XGBoost的AUROC为0.855（95%CI 0.796-0.906），AUPRC为0.332（95%CI 0.217-0.494）。standalone LLM产生1例明确的结果泄露，而agentic pipeline无泄露。在14例与SHAP审查重叠的案例中：standalone LLM的SHAP对齐度更高（0.171 vs 0.077）、方向一致性更高（92.9% vs 78.6%）；agentic pipeline的指南grounding更高（0.762 vs 0.143）、价值特异性更高（0.236 vs 0.143）、可信度略高（0.700 vs 0.671）。

Conclusion: Agentic分解可能改善安全相关的grounding和患者特异性细节，但用于高风险解释场景前应结合基于归因的检查。两种方法各有优劣，互补使用可能更佳。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [103] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了大型模型(LM)在电池健康管理(BPHM)中的应用，旨在克服传统方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法(包括物理模型和任务导向深度学习)面临计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化能力差、对大量标注失效数据的依赖以及模型可解释性不足等问题，需要新的范式来突破这些长期瓶颈。

Method: 本文系统性地审查了LM在BPHM中的应用，从四个关键维度分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化能力和鲁棒性、整合领域知识以提高可解释性、实现系统级自动化。方法包括 Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集和参数高效微调(PEFT)技术。

Result: 虽然LM在BPHM中展现出前景，但数据可获取性、智能验证、信任度和部署可行性等方面仍存在重大挑战。

Conclusion: 提出未来研究路线图：构建协作数据生态系统、验证工业应用智能、增强物理信息设计的信任度、实现高效设备端部署。为研究人员开发下一代安全、可靠、自主的电池管理系统提供系统性指导。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [104] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PICasso是一个AI辅助框架,用于从自然语言规范自动合成、验证和优化光子集成电路(PIC),通过结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈将LLM转变为实用的PIC设计代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在光子电路设计领域存在脆弱性问题,无法直接生成可制造的布局;同时缺乏标准化的基准来评估AI驱动的光子设计方法。

Method: 提出PICasso框架,包含NL→YAML→GDS生成管道、PDK感知知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS验证和SAX光子仿真;同时构建包含36个参数化PIC设计任务的PIC-Set基准测试集。

Result: 结构Spec@3达到92.7%,功能Spec@3在复杂电路上达到52%;平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB,改善1.74 dB。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈可将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理,能够以具有竞争力的运行时间生产可制造的布局。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [105] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIFQA是一个针对计算密集型金融问答的确定性工具增强多智能体框架，通过将语言理解与数值计算分离来解决大语言模型在金融多步计算中产生数值错误的问题。该框架在定期存款查询任务上实现了95.54%的计算准确率和90.87%的总体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言任务上表现强劲，但在解决多步金融计算时经常产生看似合理但数值错误的答案。金融问答涉及结构化费率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束，需要精确推理。

Method: CIFQA采用多智能体架构，将任务分配给专门智能体（查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成），同时使用确定性Python工具执行金融计算和规则应用。框架将语言理解与数值执行分离。

Result: 在定期存款查询的基准测试上，CIFQA达到95.54%的计算密集查询准确率和90.87%的总体准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线。消融研究证明确定性组件（精确费率查询、存期计算、滚动年调整和提前支取逻辑）是关键贡献因素。一个17B开源模型在CIFQA中的表现优于规模更大的前沿模型。

Conclusion: 架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性。确定性工具增强的多智能体框架能有效提升LLM在计算密集型金融推理任务中的数值准确性，该框架具有可推广到其他金融推理任务的潜力。
